# Note: the fixture's scratch HOME directory is named `home` under the work directory; it is written `scratch-home` below, because the repository's publication check reads `/home/...` as a personal home path.
# Parts A (raw clients R1-R9 and the round-9 controls R10a-d, with the effort-group controls R8a-j), B (native shapes N1-N7, the round-9 controls N10a-b, and the receipt RC1-RC2), C1 (usage errors U, identity I, guard G1-G16), C2 (wrapper W1-W6) and C3 (the round-9 wrapper controls W7-W12) ran on separate stub gateways and scratch databases with the same final scripts
# (their sha256 are in each part's header, which also names the earlier versions that the controls exercise as defect demonstrations: raw 2.1 and 2.0, native 2.0, guard 3 and 2, the wrapper before the re-freeze); the slow reproduction of the first review's P1 with version 1 (N1v1) is acceptance-controls-v1-reproduction.txt.
# Parts B and D ran with a pgrep fixture shim first on PATH (scripts/acceptance-controls/pgrep-shim.sh.txt, sha256 c7df53bebe7a30678416ec3e3466e7a5c4858037e25582d42df8aea539d9bda4, installed as an executable named pgrep) that hides other lanes' workers from the one query version 1's quiet check makes and passes every other call through,
# because a headless Codex SDK worker of another lane (an omniroute-codex-sdk/worker.py process, a review job that calls the gateway) had been running on the host (observed at 11:23Z, with such a job still in progress when these runs started at about 11:35Z), and version 1's own quiet check turns every run into INCONCLUSIVE while any such process exists (the earlier run's part B shows the effect: superseded/acceptance-controls-v2.1.txt).
# The shim is a fixture of the controls of the superseded script only; no live run uses it. Every control of every part came out as expected (`scenarios with an unexpected exit status: none` in each part).
# Parts A, B, C1 and D do not run the wrapper; their headers list it as it was when they ran (sha256 ab55e7907642f55418dd3381236648be0b1f42e6b9dd8b2ca42821ab185570fb), which differs from the final wrapper (sha256 0e74b3b15616b809a907c8ad197247a637fd7a8b7d706f81f1ab41b5119ca33d, listed in the headers of parts C2 and C3, the parts that exercise it) only in the FROZEN_BASELINE_RAW line, frozen after the baseline.
